The standard approach for minimizing regret in online prediction with expert advice is the weighted majority algorithm. Here's how it works:

- Initialize each expert's weight to 1.
- At each step, predict the majority vote of the experts, weighted by their current weights.
- If the prediction is wrong, multiply the weights of the experts who were wrong by 1/2 (or some other factor, like 1/√2 for better regret bounds).
- Repeat.
